      ;(function () {
        function applyTheme(doc) {
          try {
            var stored = localStorage.getItem('theme')
            var prefersDark = window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches
            // 'system' (or unset) follows the OS; 'dark'/'light' are explicit choices.
            var isDark = stored === 'dark' || ((stored === 'system' || stored === null) && prefersDark)
            if (isDark) {
              doc.documentElement.classList.add('dark')
            } else {
              doc.documentElement.classList.remove('dark')
            }
          } catch (e) {}
        }

        applyTheme(document)

        // The ClientRouter copies the incoming <html> attributes over the live ones, which
        // would drop the theme class until the darkmode button re-adds it. Apply the theme
        // to the incoming document so the class survives the swap without a flash.
        document.addEventListener('astro:before-swap', function (event) {
          applyTheme(event.newDocument)
        })
      })()

    </script><!-- End Google Tag Manager --><script>
      ;(function () {
        // GTM/Axeptio inject the consent banner + widget + styles into <head>/<body>
        // AFTER initial render. Astro's ClientRouter swaps <head>/<body> on
        // navigation, detaching those injected nodes: the banner disappears and
        // openAxeptioCookies() stops working (SDK DOM refs point to detached
        // nodes). A full reload re-runs GTM → re-injects Axeptio → works again.
        //
        // Fix: tag every node injected post-render, park the exact same nodes
        // under <html> (outside head/body) during the swap, then move them back
        // after. Nodes stay in the same document throughout — no cross-document
        // adoption — so the Axeptio SDK's JS references remain valid. Scripts
        // are skipped so the SDK is never re-executed (no duplicate banner).
        var PERSIST = 'data-consent-persist'
        var PARK = 'data-consent-park'
        var observer = null

        function tag(node) {
          if (!node || node.nodeType !== 1) return
          if (node.tagName === 'SCRIPT') return
          if (node.hasAttribute(PERSIST) || node.hasAttribute(PARK)) return
          // Don't tag Astro's own transition/runtime nodes
          if (node.id === 'main' || node.id === 'mistral-data') return
          if (node.tagName === 'HEADER' || node.tagName === 'MAIN' || node.tagName === 'FOOTER') return
          node.setAttribute(PERSIST, '')
        }

        function observeTarget(target) {
          if (!target || !observer) return
          observer.observe(target, { childList: true })
        }

        // Start after DOM is parsed so we don't tag server-rendered head/body
        // elements still being added by the HTML parser. Axeptio is injected
        // by GTM via requestIdleCallback, which fires after DOMContentLoaded.
        function startObserver() {
          if (observer) return
          observer = new MutationObserver(function (records) {
            for (var i = 0; i < records.length; i++) {
              var added = records[i].addedNodes
              for (var j = 0; j < added.length; j++) tag(added[j])
            }
          })
          observeTarget(document.head)
          observeTarget(document.body)
        }

        if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
          document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', startObserver)
        } else {
          startObserver()
        }

        // Park tagged nodes under <html> so they survive the head/body swap.
        document.addEventListener('astro:before-swap', function () {
          var nodes = document.querySelectorAll('[' + PERSIST + ']')
          for (var i = 0; i < nodes.length; i++) {
            var n = nodes[i]
            if (!n.parentNode) continue
            n.setAttribute(PARK, document.head.contains(n) ? 'head' : 'body')
            document.documentElement.appendChild(n)
          }
        })

        // Move parked nodes back into head or body.
        document.addEventListener('astro:after-swap', function () {
          var parked = document.querySelectorAll('[' + PARK + ']')
          for (var i = 0; i < parked.length; i++) {
            var n = parked[i]
            var origin = n.getAttribute(PARK)
            n.removeAttribute(PARK)
            if (origin === 'head' || n.tagName === 'STYLE' || n.tagName === 'LINK' || n.tagName === 'META') {
              document.head.appendChild(n)
            } else {
              document.body.appendChild(n)
            }
          }
          // Re-observe body in case it was replaced
          if (observer) observeTarget(document.body)
        })
      })()

The response to the preview has exceeded our expectations, and we&#39;re grateful to our developer community, early-access customers, and partners for their support, collaboration, and feedback.</p><figure class="mb-10"><img src="/_astro/partners-ml4-oss_17QG5o.webp?dpl=6ae3844da0718a08567e65ba" srcset="/_astro/partners-ml4-oss_Z2rKs4X.webp?dpl=6ae3844da0718a08567e65ba 400w, /_astro/partners-ml4-oss_Z1FWpb7.webp?dpl=6ae3844da0718a08567e65ba 768w, /_astro/partners-ml4-oss_XH2km.webp?dpl=6ae3844da0718a08567e65ba 1280w, /_astro/partners-ml4-oss_Z17Vyq1.webp?dpl=6ae3844da0718a08567e65ba 1920w" alt sizes="(max-width: 640px) 400px, (max-width: 1024px) 768px, (max-width: 1440px) 1280px, 1920px" loading="lazy" decoding="async" width="2400" height="1160" class="w-full"></figure><p class="text-body-large text-text-primary mb-10">Since then, we&#39;ve put that feedback to work while continuing to scale reinforcement learning, driving massive improvements in <strong><em>coding</em></strong> and defensive <strong><em>cybersecurity</em></strong>. Though we observed the model refusing to comply with black-box attacks against live targets, we see a potential for uplift to conduct real attacks. We are modifying our guardrails accordingly, and will continue updating them in the months to come. </p><p class="text-body-large text-text-primary mb-10">We are also providing enterprise design partners such as Orange Cyberdefense, with preview access to a specialized variant of Mistral Large 4 optimized for advanced cybersecurity workflows, including penetration testing, vulnerability assessment, and red-teaming. Access is subject to rigorous vetting and contractual safeguards, with testing restricted to owned or explicitly authorized systems in controlled environments.</p><p class="text-body-large text-text-primary mb-10">Meanwhile, customers are tailoring Mistral Large 4 with proprietary security data, policies, detection logic, and threat environments using Mistral <a href="https://mistral.ai/products/forge/" class="underline font-semibold"><u>Forge</u></a>. For well-defined cybersecurity tasks, it can be further optimized into smaller, task-specific variants for greater accuracy, making security applications more effective.</p><p class="text-body-large text-text-primary mb-10"><a href="/industry/cybersecurity" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="underline font-semibold">Learn more about sovereign AI for cybersecurity and how to work with us.</a></p><h2 class="text-h2 font-mistral text-current mb-5 pb-5 border-b border-border-primary not-first:mt-16">Built for Work.</h2><p class="text-body-large text-text-primary mb-10">Mistral Large 4 has continued improving on a range of broad enterprise-use metrics. </p><p class="text-body-large text-text-primary mb-10"><br><strong>For finance-related work</strong>, we improved the model’s ability to perform real-world workflows, especially ones involving the creation and editing of spreadsheets for finance and accounting use cases. </span></div><div class="w-full p-8 md:p-20 flex flex-col gap-10 border border-border-primary bg-surface-brand-secondary"><p class="text-h4 font-mistral text-current">This major investment by Mistral AI reinforces the choice La Banque Postale made over a year ago and underscores the relevance of the long-term strategic partnership we are building together: supporting the emergence of an open and sovereign European AI leader. For a public and citizen bank, technological sovereignty is not optional; it is a key prerequisite for maintaining our customers&#39; trust. The introduction of this open model unlocks new opportunities and use cases. By partnering with Mistral AI, our ambition is to combine the best of human expertise and digital technology to deliver an enhanced experience of both customers and employees over the long term.</p><p class="text-body-base text-current">Zakaria Moursli, Executive Vice President, Operational Performance, Payments and Digital Services, La Banque Postale</p></div><div class="flex items-end"><div class="size-10 md:size-17 shrink-0 bg-surface-brand-tertiary border border-border-primary"></div></div></div><h2 class="text-h2 font-mistral text-current mb-5 pb-5 border-b border-border-primary not-first:mt-16">See it in Action</h2><p class="text-body-large text-text-primary mb-10"><u>Cyber Vulnerability Triage</u>: an agent reads incoming vulnerability reports, investigates the source code and product context, reproduces the flaws in an isolated sandbox, then writes and iterates on a patch until a canary exploit no longer works.</p><p class="text-body-large text-text-primary mb-10"></p><section class="mb-10"><mistral-block-iframe-auto class="w-full block"><iframe src="https://ml4-blog-widgets.netlify.app/django-rediscovery/?iframe" class="w-full block border-0 overflow-hidden" allowfullscreen loading="lazy" referrerpolicy="strict-origin-when-cross-origin"></iframe></mistral-block-iframe-auto></section><p class="text-body-large text-text-primary mb-10"></p><p class="text-body-large text-text-primary mb-10"><u>Agentic webdev: </u>with no human help, Mistral Large 4 builds Orbital Trace, a live 3D map of roughly 19,000 satellites and debris pieces orbiting Earth. It pulls real tracking data, writes the app, uses it in a browser to catch its own bugs and slowdowns, then fixes what an independent reviewer flags until all requirements pass.</p><p class="text-body-large text-text-primary mb-10"></p><p class="text-body-large text-text-primary mb-10"></p><section class="mb-10"><mistral-block-iframe-auto class="w-full block"><iframe src="https://ml4-blog-widgets.netlify.app/agentic-frontend-coding/?iframe" class="w-full block border-0 overflow-hidden" allowfullscreen loading="lazy" referrerpolicy="strict-origin-when-cross-origin"></iframe></mistral-block-iframe-auto></section><p class="text-body-large text-text-primary mb-10"></p><p class="text-body-large text-text-primary mb-10"><u>Quantum chemistry solver:</u> Mistral Large 4 writes, from scratch, software that calculates where electrons sit in a molecule, and checks its answers against a standard chemistry tool. It then builds a 3D page around it: pick a molecule such as benzene or water, drag a slider to pull it apart, and watch the electrons regroup as the bond breaks.</p><p class="text-body-large text-text-primary mb-10"></p><section class="mb-10"><mistral-block-iframe-auto class="w-full block"><iframe src="https://ml4-blog-widgets.netlify.app/bond-breaking/?iframe" class="w-full block border-0 overflow-hidden" allowfullscreen loading="lazy" referrerpolicy="strict-origin-when-cross-origin"></iframe></mistral-block-iframe-auto></section><p class="text-body-large text-text-primary mb-10"></p><p class="text-body-large text-text-primary mb-10"></p><h2 class="text-h2 font-mistral text-current mb-5 pb-5 border-b border-border-primary not-first:mt-16">Built for Sovereignty.</h2><p class="text-body-large text-text-primary mb-10">As organizations build AI around their knowledge and workflows, sovereignty means that they retain control of the models they depend on and the intelligence they create. </p><p class="text-body-large text-text-primary mb-10">With Mistral Large 4, that control begins with the model itself. Organizations have the ability to inspect, customize, and deploy the model on their own infrastructure. They can use and improve Mistral Large 4 without depending on Mistral to serve it through an API. Mistral Large 4 was developed and trained from scratch in Europe, giving organizations clarity about the origins of the technology they’re building on. </p><p class="text-body-large text-text-primary mb-10">Control also extends to how AI operates inside an organization. Organizations can customize Mistral Large 4 around their proprietary data with Mistral <a href="https://mistral.ai/products/forge/" class="underline font-semibold"><u>Forge</u></a>, and they can build, govern, and improve the resulting agents and applications using Mistral <a href="https://mistral.ai/products/studio/" class="underline font-semibold"><u>Studio.</u></a> Mistral’s Applied AI experts can work alongside customer teams to build and deploy these tailored systems. Through that work, teams understand the architecture and decisions behind what they’ve built, along with the methods used to train and operate it, so they can run and improve their AI independently. </p><mistral-node-quote-carousel data-astro-cid-mqtyz4oa="true"><div class="relative flex flex-col lg:flex-col-reverse lg:pb-nav pb-6 overflow-x-hidden border-y border-border-primary" data-astro-cid-mqtyz4oa><div class="w-full js-slider relative z-2overflow-hidden" data-astro-cid-mqtyz4oa><div class="relative z-2 w-full flex gap-0 js-slider-wrapper" data-astro-cid-mqtyz4oa><div data-active="true" class="js-slider-item duration-300 transition-all bg-surface-brand-primary z-1 relative w-full h-auto shrink-0 flex flex-col-reverse lg:flex-row items-start justify-start" data-astro-cid-mqtyz4oa><div class="w-full js-content h-full duration-300 transition-all flex flex-col relative lg:pt-30" data-astro-cid-mqtyz4oa><div class="flex flex-col gap-4 lg:gap-10 w-full h-full justify-between" data-astro-cid-mqtyz4oa><p class="text-h4 font-mistral text-current" data-astro-cid-mqtyz4oa="true">At ABN AMRO, we believe that open and transparent AI ecosystems are essential for driving innovation responsibly within the financial sector. Developments such as Mistral Large 4 demonstrate how sovereign and open-weight AI models can expand the range of trusted solutions available to European organizations. We are encouraged by the progress being made and continue to explore how advanced AI can contribute to strengthening critical capabilities, including cybersecurity.</p><div class="flex flex-col gap-4" </span></div><div class="w-full p-8 md:p-20 flex flex-col gap-10 border border-border-primary bg-surface-brand-secondary"><p class="text-h4 font-mistral text-current">Microsoft and Mistral AI share a commitment to expanding customer choice in next-generation AI. As the first cloud provider to bring Mistral Large 4 to customers, Microsoft is making Mistral’s latest open-weight model available through Microsoft Foundry, an enterprise AI platform built for evaluating, deploying, and operating models at scale. For organizations advancing sovereign AI strategies, this combination offers greater flexibility and control across their data, infrastructure, and AI operations.</p><p class="text-body-base text-current">Tina Schuchman, Corporate Vice President, Foundry, Microsoft </p></div><div class="flex items-end"><div class="size-10 md:size-17 shrink-0 bg-surface-brand-tertiary border border-border-primary"></div></div></div><h2 class="text-h2 font-mistral text-current mb-5 pb-5 border-b border-border-primary not-first:mt-16">Start building with Le Chonk</h2><p class="text-body-large text-text-primary mb-10">Mistral Large 4 is available to try, deploy, and customize today.</p><p class="text-body-large text-text-primary mb-10">Get started in <a href="https://console.mistral.ai/home" class="underline font-semibold"><u>Mistral Studio</u></a>, through the Mistral <a href="https://docs.mistral.ai/models/mistral-large-4-0#use-mistral-large-4-0" class="underline font-semibold"><u>API</u></a>, or on <a href="https://openrouter.ai/mistralai/mistral-large-4-0" class="underline font-semibold"><u>OpenRouter</u></a>. For full control, download the open weights from <a href="https://huggingface.co/mistralai/Mistral-Large-4-1T-A52B" class="underline font-semibold"><u>Hugging Face</u></a> and deploy on your own infrastructure with vLLM or SGLang. The <a href="https://docs.mistral.ai/models/mistral-large-4-0" class="underline font-semibold"><u>model card</u></a> and <a href="https://huggingface.co/mistralai/Mistral-Large-4-1T-A52B/blob/main/LICENSE" class="underline font-semibold"><u>license</u></a> have everything you need to get started — and for those who want to go deeper, a full technical report is coming soon.</p><p class="text-body-large text-text-primary mb-10">You can also try the new model in <a href="https://chat.mistral.ai/code?model=mistral-large-4-0" class="underline font-semibold"><u>Vibe Code</u></a>; give it a feature to build or a bug to fix in your own repository.</p><p class="text-body-large text-text-primary mb-10">Mistral Large 4, is also available through <a href="https://build.nvidia.com/mistralai/mistral-large-4-1t-a52b" class="underline font-semibold"><u>NVIDIA-hosted endpoints</u></a>, as an optimized <a href="https://catalog.ngc.nvidia.com/orgs/nim/nvidia/containers/vllm-model-free-nim-experimental/-" class="underline font-semibold"><u>NVIDIA NIM container</u></a> for serving with vLLM. Developers can use the open source <a href="https://github.com/NVIDIA-NeMo/Automodel/blob/main/docs/model-coverage/vlm/mistralai/mistral-large-4.mdx" class="underline font-semibold"><u>NeMo AutoModel library</u></a> for customization or the open source <a href="https://github.com/NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM/tree/feat/mistral-large-4" class="underline font-semibold"><u>TensorRT LLM</u></a> library for high-throughput serving.</p><p class="text-body-large text-text-primary mb-10">Enterprises can also tailor Mistral Large 4 to their proprietary data, use cases, and guardrails with Mistral Forge. <a href="https://mistral.ai/contact/" class="underline font-semibold"><u>Talk to our team</u></a> to learn more.</p><p class="text-body-large text-text-primary mb-10">The model is available to use under the <a href="https://huggingface.co/mistralai/Mistral-Large-4-1T-A52B/blob/main/LICENSE" class="underline font-semibold"><em>Mistral Ecosystem Open-Weight license</em></a><em>, </em>a modified MIT license. The model is free to use, modify, fine-tune, and commercialize, with a single condition: businesses that operate a Model-as-a-Service offering must obtain a separate commercial license from Mistral before using the model commercially.</p><p class="text-body-large text-text-primary mb-10">Starting today, Mistral Large 4 is priced at <strong><em>$0.70 input</em></strong> / M, <strong><em>$0.07 cached input</em></strong> / M, and <strong><em>$2.10 output</em></strong> / M tokens.</p><p class="text-body-large text-text-primary mb-10">We encourage the community to put Mistral Large 4 to the test. Run your own evaluations, push its limits, and share your findings with us on <a href="https://x.com/MistralAI?lang=en" class="underline font-semibold"><u>X</u></a>, <a href="https://discord.com/invite/mistralai" class="underline font-semibold"><u>Discord</u></a>, or <a href="https://github.com/mistralai" class="underline font-semibold"><u>GitHub</u></a>.</p><h2 class="text-h2 font-mistral text-current mb-5 pb-5 border-b border-border-primary not-first:mt-16">What&#39;s Next</h2><p class="text-body-large text-text-primary mb-10">Mistral Large 4 will serve as the foundation for a new generation of specialized and optimized Mistral models. Building on recent releases such as <a href="https://mistral.ai/news/voxtral-3/" class="underline font-semibold"><u>Voxtral</u></a> and <a href="https://mistral.ai/news/timestral/" class="underline font-semibold"><u>Timestral</u></a>, we&#39;ll continue expanding the frontier of open-weight AI, continuing to give developers and enterprises greater freedom to build, customize, and own their intelligence.</p></section></div><div class="w-full lg:w-96 shrink-0 xl:border-r lg:border-l border-border-primary"><div class="sticky top-12 lg:pl-6 py-4 xl:px-4 flex flex-col gap-4
